    TheoremThe canonical move follows the bespoke strategy at Verifier positions with a move#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.canonicalNext_pos_of_owner

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\mathit{env}\) an environment and \(\sigma\) a Verifier strategy for the model-checking game on \(M\). Write \(\langle \psi, v \rangle\) for the game position with the embedding of a fixpoint-free formula \(\psi\), the world \(v\) and the empty binder stack. Let \(\varphi\) be a fixpoint-free formula and \(w\) a world such that Verifier owns \(\langle \varphi, w \rangle\) and \(\langle \varphi, w \rangle\) has a move to some \(\langle \varphi'', w'' \rangle\). Then there are a fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi'\) and a world \(w'\) such that the canonical next move induced by \(\sigma\) (in environment \(\mathit{env}\)) sends the fragment position \((\varphi, w)\) to the fragment position \((\varphi', w')\), and \(\sigma\) chooses \(\langle \varphi', w' \rangle\) at \(\langle \varphi, w \rangle\).

    theorem canonicalNext_pos_of_owner {M : KripkeModel World Atom}
    {env : ℕ → World → Prop} {σ : Strategy M} {φ : FixFreeFormula Atom}
    {w : World}
    (hOwn : Owner ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ = true)
    (hExists : ∃ φ' w', Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
      ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩) :
    ∃ φ' w', canonicalNext M env σ (.pos φ w) = .pos φ' w' ∧
      σ.choose ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ = ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩
    structure KripkeModel (World : Type u) (Atom : Type v) where
  /-- There is at least one world. -/
  nonempty_world : Nonempty World
  /-- The accessibility relation. -/
  rel : World → World → Prop
  /-- The atoms true at each world. -/
  valuation : World → Atom → Prop

      Move M ⟨.and φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨ψ, w, st⟩
  /-- Verifier chooses a disjunct. -/
  | orLeft {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.or φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨φ, w, st⟩
  /-- Verifier chooses a disjunct. -/
  ⋯

    noncomputable def canonicalNext (M : KripkeModel World Atom)
    (env : ℕ → World → Prop) (σ : Strategy M) : FragPos Atom World → FragPos Atom World
  | .win => .win
  | .lose => .lose
  | .pos φ w =>
      if hExists : ∃ φ' w', Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
          ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩ then

    DefinitionPositional strategy induced by a bespoke strategy#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragStrategy

    The canonical positional strategy induced by a bespoke strategy.

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragStrategy · 60 lines 
    noncomputable def fragStrategy (M : KripkeModel World Atom)
    (env : ℕ → World → Prop) (σ : Strategy M) :
    (fragmentGame M env).PositionalStrategy Cslib.Games.Player.even where
  next := canonicalNext M env σ
  legal := by
    intro v hv
    cases v with
    | win =>
        show FragMove M env .win (canonicalNext M env σ .win)
        rw [show canonicalNext M env σ (FragPos.win : FragPos Atom World) = .win from rfl]
        exact FragMove.win_self
    | lose =>
        show FragMove M env .lose (canonicalNext M env σ .lose)
        rw [show canonicalNext M env σ (FragPos.lose : FragPos Atom World) = .lose from rfl]
        exact FragMove.lose_self
    | pos φ w =>
        have hOwn : Owner ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ = true :=
          (fragOwner_pos_eq_even).mp hv
        show FragMove M env (.pos φ w) (canonicalNext M env σ (.pos φ w))
        by_cases hExists : ∃ φ' w', Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
            ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩
        · obtain ⟨φ', w', hNext, heq⟩ := canonicalNext_pos_of_owner hOwn hExists
          obtain ⟨φ₀, w₀, h₀⟩ := hExists
          have hChoose : Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
              ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩ := by
            rw [← heq]
            exact σ.choose_move _ hOwn ⟨⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ₀, w₀, []⟩, h₀⟩
          rw [hNext]
          exact FragMove.pos_move hChoose
        · have hNo : NoMoves M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ := by
            intro q hq
            obtain ⟨φ', w', hq', -⟩ := move_of_toMu hq
            exact hExists ⟨φ', w', by rwa [hq'] at hq⟩
          by_cases hWin : TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
          · have hNext : canonicalNext M env σ (.pos φ w) = FragPos.win := by
              classical
              show (if h : ∃ φ' w', Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
                  ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩ then
                (if Owner ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ = true then
                  fragOfGamePos (σ.choose ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩)
                else .pos (Classical.choose h)
                  (Classical.choose (Classical.choose_spec h)))
                else if TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ then
                  FragPos.win else FragPos.lose) = FragPos.win
              rw [dite_eq_right hExists, ite_eq_left hWin]
            rw [hNext]
            exact FragMove.toWin hNo hWin
          · have hNext : canonicalNext M env σ (.pos φ w) = FragPos.lose := by
              classical
              show (if h : ∃ φ' w', Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
                  ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩ then
                (if Owner ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ = true then
                  fragOfGamePos (σ.choose ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩)
                else .pos (Classical.choose h)
                  (Classical.choose (Classical.choose_spec h)))
                else if TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ then
                  FragPos.win else FragPos.lose) = FragPos.lose
              rw [dite_eq_right hExists, ite_eq_right hWin]
            rw [hNext]
            exact FragMove.toLose hNo hWin

    DefinitionGame position of a fragment position#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragToPos

    Given a default game position \(p_0\), an embedded fragment position \((\varphi, w)\) is sent to the game position with formula \(\varphi\), world \(w\) and empty binder stack, and the absorbing winning and losing states are both sent to \(p_0\).

    The default is never used on plays that stay among embedded positions.

    def fragToPos (p0 : GamePosition Atom World) : FragPos Atom World → GamePosition Atom World
  | .pos φ w => ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
  | _ => p0
    abbrev GamePosition (Atom World : Type u) :=
  MuFormula Atom × World × List (MuFormula Atom)

    def toMu : FixFreeFormula Atom → MuFormula Atom
  | .atom a => .atom a
  | .natom a => .natom a
  | .bot => .bot
  | .top => .top
  | .and φ ψ => .and (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .or φ ψ => .or (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .box φ => .box (toMu φ)
  | .dia φ => .dia (toMu φ)

    DefinitionSegment of a fragment play as game positions#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragSegment

    Let \(p_0\) be a game position and let \(\pi = \pi_0 \pi_1 \pi_2 \dots\) be an infinite sequence of positions of the totalized fragment game. For natural numbers \(s\) and \(k\), the segment of \(\pi\) from \(s\) of length \(k + 1\) is the list of game positions obtained from \(\pi_s, \pi_{s+1}, \dots, \pi_{s+k}\), in this order, by sending each embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) to the game position with formula \(\varphi\), world \(w\) and empty binder stack, and each of the absorbing winning and losing states to \(p_0\).

    def fragSegment (p0 : GamePosition Atom World) (π : ωSequence (FragPos Atom World))
    (start : ℕ) : ℕ → List (GamePosition Atom World)
  | 0 => [fragToPos p0 (π start)]
  | k + 1 => fragToPos p0 (π start) :: fragSegment p0 π (start + 1) k
    abbrev GamePosition (Atom World : Type u) :=
  MuFormula Atom × World × List (MuFormula Atom)

    TheoremSplitting off the head of a segment#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragSegment_cons

    Let \(p_0\) be a model-checking game position and \(\pi\) an infinite sequence of positions of the totalized fragment game. For each \(i\), let \(\hat\pi_i\) be \(p_0\) if \(\pi_i\) is an absorbing state; if \(\pi_i\) is the embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) of a fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi\) and a world \(w\), let \(\hat\pi_i\) be the game position \((\varphi, w, [])\), with \(\varphi\) embedded as a μ-calculus formula and the empty fixpoint stack. For all natural numbers \(s\) and \(k\), the segment \([\hat\pi_s, \dots, \hat\pi_{s+k+1}]\) of \(\pi\) is the list with head \(\hat\pi_s\) followed by the segment \([\hat\pi_{s+1}, \dots, \hat\pi_{s+k+1}]\).

    theorem fragSegment_cons (p0 : GamePosition Atom World)
    (π : ωSequence (FragPos Atom World)) (start k : ℕ) :
    fragSegment p0 π start (k + 1) =
      fragToPos p0 (π start) :: fragSegment p0 π (start + 1) k

    TheoremSegments of canonical plays through fragment positions are bespoke plays#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.canonical_playFrom_segment

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\mathit{env}\) an environment and \(\sigma\) a Verifier strategy for the model-checking game on \(M\). Write \(\langle \psi, v \rangle\) for the game position with the embedding of a fixpoint-free formula \(\psi\), the world \(v\) and the empty binder stack. Let \(\pi\) be a play of the fragment game of \(M\) and \(\mathit{env}\) that conforms to the canonical positional strategy induced by \(\sigma\), and let \(s, k \in \mathbb{N}\). Suppose every \(\pi_i\) with \(s \le i \le s + k\) is a fragment position \((\varphi_i, w_i)\), not \(\mathrm{win}\) or \(\mathrm{lose}\). Then the list \(\langle \varphi_s, w_s \rangle, \langle \varphi_{s+1}, w_{s+1} \rangle, \dots, \langle \varphi_{s+k}, w_{s+k} \rangle\) is a finite play from \(\langle \varphi_s, w_s \rangle\) consistent with \(\sigma\): each position has a game move to the next, and at Verifier-owned positions the next position is the one \(\sigma\) chooses.

    theorem canonical_playFrom_segment {M : KripkeModel World Atom}
    {env : ℕ → World → Prop} {σ : Strategy M} {π : ωSequence (FragPos Atom World)}
    (p0 : GamePosition Atom World) (start k : ℕ)
    (φ : FixFreeFormula Atom) (w : World)
    (hplay : (fragmentGame M env).IsPlay π)
    (hconf : Cslib.Games.ParityGame.Conforms (fragStrategy M env σ) π)
    (hstart : π start = .pos φ w)
    (hpos : ∀ i, start ≤ i → i ≤ start + k → ∃ φ' w', π i = .pos φ' w') :
    PlayFrom M σ ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ (fragSegment p0 π start k)

    inductive PlayFrom (M : KripkeModel World Atom) (σ : Strategy M) :
    GamePosition Atom World → List (GamePosition Atom World) → Prop
  /-- The trivial play. -/
  | single {p : GamePosition Atom World} : PlayFrom M σ p [p]
  /-- A move followed by a play from the successor. -/
  | cons {p q : GamePosition Atom World} {ps : List (GamePosition Atom World)}
      (h : Move M p q) (hσ : Owner p = true → q = σ.choose p) (hps : PlayFrom M σ q ps) :
      PlayFrom M σ p (p :: ps)

    TheoremPlays of the totalized fragment game stay at the winning state#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragPlay_eq_win_of_le

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model and \(\mathit{env}\) an environment assigning a set of worlds to each free-variable index. If \(\pi\) is a play of the totalized fragment game of \(M\) and \(\mathit{env}\) and \(\pi_k\) is the absorbing winning state, then \(\pi_n\) is the winning state for every \(n \ge k\).

    π n = .win

    TheoremLast position of a segment#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragSegment_playLast

    Let \(p_0\) be a model-checking game position and \(\pi\) an infinite sequence of positions of the totalized fragment game. For each \(i\), let \(\hat\pi_i\) be \(p_0\) if \(\pi_i\) is an absorbing state; if \(\pi_i\) is the embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) of a fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi\) and a world \(w\), let \(\hat\pi_i\) be the game position \((\varphi, w, [])\), with \(\varphi\) embedded as a μ-calculus formula and the empty fixpoint stack. For all natural numbers \(s\) and \(k\), the segment \([\hat\pi_s, \dots, \hat\pi_{s+k}]\) has a last position, and it is \(\hat\pi_{s+k}\).

    theorem fragSegment_playLast (p0 : GamePosition Atom World)
    (π : ωSequence (FragPos Atom World)) (start k : ℕ) :
    playLast (fragSegment p0 π start k) = some (fragToPos p0 (π (start + k)))
    abbrev GamePosition (Atom World : Type u) :=
  MuFormula Atom × World × List (MuFormula Atom)

    TheoremWinning Verifier strategies transfer to the fragment game#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragment_bespoke_to_canonical

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\rho\) an environment, \(\sigma\) a Verifier strategy of the model-checking game on \(M\), \(\varphi\) a fixpoint-free formula and \(w\) a world. If \(\sigma\) wins in environment \(\rho\) from the game position with formula \(\varphi\), world \(w\) and empty binder stack, then the positional strategy of the even player induced by \(\sigma\) wins from the embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) in the fragment game of \(M\) and \(\rho\).

    Every play conforming to the induced strategy reaches the absorbing winning state: it cannot reach the losing state, by the finite-play clause for \(\sigma\) applied to the segment before the first losing state, and it cannot stay among embedded positions forever, since fragment moves strictly decrease the formula.

    theorem fragment_bespoke_to_canonical {M : KripkeModel World Atom}
    {env : ℕ → World → Prop} {σ : Strategy M} {φ : FixFreeFormula Atom}
    {w : World}
    (hσ : StrategyWins M env σ ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩) :
    (fragmentGame M env).WinsFrom (fragStrategy M env σ)
      (.pos φ w)
    structure KripkeModel (World : Type u) (Atom : Type v) where
  /-- There is at least one world. -/
  nonempty_world : Nonempty World
  /-- The accessibility relation. -/
  rel : World → World → Prop
  /-- The atoms true at each world. -/
  valuation : World → Atom → Prop

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/Kripke.lean:83

    structure Strategy (M : KripkeModel World Atom) where
  /-- The chosen successor at a Verifier-owned position. -/
  choose : GamePosition Atom World → GamePosition Atom World
  /-- The chosen successor is a legal move whenever one exists. -/
  choose_move : ∀ p, Owner p = true → (∃ q, Move M p q) → Move M p (choose p)

    inductive FixFreeFormula (Atom : Type u) : Type u where
  /-- An atom. -/
  | atom : Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- A negated atom. -/
  | natom : Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Falsity. -/
  | bot : FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Truth. -/
  | top : FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Conjunction. -/
  | and : FixFreeFormula Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Disjunction. -/
  ⋯

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:515

    def StrategyWins (M : KripkeModel World Atom) (env : ℕ → World → Prop)
    (σ : Strategy M) (p : GamePosition Atom World) : Prop :=
  (∀ ps : List (GamePosition Atom World), PlayFrom M σ p ps →
    (∃ q, playLast ps = some q ∧ NoMoves M q) →
      ∃ q, playLast ps = some q ∧ TerminalWin M env q) ∧
  (∀ play : ℕ → GamePosition Atom World, play 0 = p →
    (∀ n, Move M (play n) (play (n + 1)) ∧
      (Owner (play n) = true → play (n + 1) = σ.choose (play n))) →
      ParityWin play)

  | .atom a => .atom a
  | .natom a => .natom a
  | .bot => .bot
  | .top => .top
  | .and φ ψ => .and (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .or φ ψ => .or (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .box φ => .box (toMu φ)
  | .dia φ => .dia (toMu φ)

    TheoremMoves of the totalized fragment game, by cases#✓fully proved

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragMove_cases

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model and \(e\) an environment, and read each fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi\) as a μ-calculus formula. Every move from \(a\) to \(b\) in the totalized fragment game of \(M\) and \(e\) is of one of five kinds: \(a\) and \(b\) are embedded positions \((\varphi, w)\) and \((\varphi', w')\) and the model-checking game of \(M\) has a move from \((\varphi, w)\) to \((\varphi', w')\) with empty binder stacks; or \(a = (\varphi, w)\), \(b\) is the winning state, and the game position \((\varphi, w)\) with empty stack has no moves and is won by Verifier in environment \(e\); or \(a = (\varphi, w)\), \(b\) is the losing state, and that game position has no moves and is not won by Verifier; or \(a\) and \(b\) are both the winning state; or \(a\) and \(b\) are both the losing state.

    theorem fragMove_cases {M : KripkeModel World Atom} {env : ℕ → World → Prop}
    {a b : FragPos Atom World} (h : FragMove M env a b) :
    (∃ φ w φ' w', a = .pos φ w ∧ b = .pos φ' w' ∧
      Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
        ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩) ∨
    (∃ φ w, a = .pos φ w ∧ b = .win ∧
      NoMoves M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ ∧
      TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩) ∨
    (∃ φ w, a = .pos φ w ∧ b = .lose ∧
      NoMoves M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩ ∧
      ¬ TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩) ∨
    (a = .win ∧ b = .win) ∨ (a = .lose ∧ b = .lose)
    structure KripkeModel (World : Type u) (Atom : Type v) where
  /-- There is at least one world. -/
  nonempty_world : Nonempty World
  /-- The accessibility relation. -/
  rel : World → World → Prop
  /-- The atoms true at each world. -/
  valuation : World → Atom → Prop

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:1205

    inductive FragMove (M : KripkeModel World Atom) (env : ℕ → World → Prop) :
    FragPos Atom World → FragPos Atom World → Prop
  /-- A game move between embedded fragment positions. -/
  | pos_move {φ φ' : FixFreeFormula Atom} {w w' : World}
      (h : Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
        ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩) :
      FragMove M env (.pos φ w) (.pos φ' w')
  /-- A terminal winning position moves to the absorbing win state. -/
  | toWin {φ : FixFreeFormula Atom} {w : World}
      (hNo : NoMoves M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩)
      (hWin : TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩) :
      FragMove M env (.pos φ w) .win
  ⋯

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:1235

    inductive Move (M : KripkeModel World Atom) :
    GamePosition Atom World → GamePosition Atom World → Prop
  /-- Refuter chooses a conjunct. -/
  | andLeft {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.and φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨φ, w, st⟩
  /-- Refuter chooses a conjunct. -/
  | andRight {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.and φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨ψ, w, st⟩
  /-- Verifier chooses a disjunct. -/
  | orLeft {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.or φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨φ, w, st⟩
  /-- Verifier chooses a disjunct. -/
  ⋯

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:116

    def toMu : FixFreeFormula Atom → MuFormula Atom
  | .atom a => .atom a
  | .natom a => .natom a
  | .bot => .bot
  | .top => .top
  | .and φ ψ => .and (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .or φ ψ => .or (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .box φ => .box (toMu φ)
  | .dia φ => .dia (toMu φ)

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:536

    def NoMoves (M : KripkeModel World Atom) (p : GamePosition Atom World) : Prop :=
  ∀ q, ¬ Move M p q

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:174

    def TerminalWin (M : KripkeModel World Atom) (env : ℕ → World → Prop) :
    GamePosition Atom World → Prop
  | ⟨.atom a, w, _⟩ => M.valuation w a
  | ⟨.natom a, w, _⟩ => ¬ M.valuation w a
  | ⟨.var n, w, st⟩ => if n < st.length then False else env (n - st.length) w
  | ⟨.top, _, _⟩ => True
  | ⟨.bot, _, _⟩ => False
  | ⟨.box _, _, _⟩ => True
  | ⟨.dia _, _, _⟩ => False
  | _ => False

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.canonical_next_pos

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\mathit{env}\) an environment, and \(\sigma\) any positional strategy of the even player (Verifier) in the fragment game of \(M\) and \(\mathit{env}\). Write \(\langle \psi, v \rangle\) for the game position with the embedding of a fixpoint-free formula \(\psi\), the world \(v\) and the empty binder stack. Let \(\varphi\) be a fixpoint-free formula and \(w\) a world such that the even player owns the fragment position \((\varphi, w)\) and, in the model-checking game on \(M\), \(\langle \varphi, w \rangle\) has a move to some \(\langle \varphi'', w'' \rangle\). Then \(\sigma\) sends \((\varphi, w)\) to a fragment position \((\varphi', w')\) such that \(\langle \varphi, w \rangle\) has a move to \(\langle \varphi', w' \rangle\); in particular \(\sigma\) does not move to the absorbing state \(\mathrm{win}\) or \(\mathrm{lose}\).

    theorem canonical_next_pos {M : KripkeModel World Atom} {env : ℕ → World → Prop}
    {σ : (fragmentGame M env).PositionalStrategy Cslib.Games.Player.even}
    {φ : FixFreeFormula Atom} {w : World}
    (hown : (fragmentGame M env).owner (.pos φ w) = Cslib.Games.Player.even)
    (hExists : ∃ φ' w', Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
      ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩) :
    ∃ φ' w', σ.next (.pos φ w) = .pos φ' w' ∧
      Move M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩
        ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ', w', []⟩
    structure KripkeModel (World : Type u) (Atom : Type v) where
  /-- There is at least one world. -/
  nonempty_world : Nonempty World
  /-- The accessibility relation. -/
  rel : World → World → Prop
  /-- The atoms true at each world. -/
  valuation : World → Atom → Prop

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/Kripke.lean:83

    noncomputable def fragmentGame (M : KripkeModel World Atom)
    (env : ℕ → World → Prop) : Cslib.Games.ParityGame (FragPos Atom World) where
  owner := fragOwner
  Move := FragMove M env
  priority := fragPriority
  serial := by
    intro v
    cases v with
    | win => exact ⟨.win, FragMove.win_self⟩
    | lose => exact ⟨.lose, FragMove.lose_self⟩
    | pos φ w =>
        by_cases hExists : ∃ φ' w', Move M
  ⋯

    inductive FixFreeFormula (Atom : Type u) : Type u where
  /-- An atom. -/
  | atom : Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- A negated atom. -/
  | natom : Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Falsity. -/
  | bot : FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Truth. -/
  | top : FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Conjunction. -/
  | and : FixFreeFormula Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Disjunction. -/

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:515

    inductive Move (M : KripkeModel World Atom) :
    GamePosition Atom World → GamePosition Atom World → Prop
  /-- Refuter chooses a conjunct. -/
  | andLeft {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.and φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨φ, w, st⟩
  /-- Refuter chooses a conjunct. -/
  | andRight {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.and φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨ψ, w, st⟩
  /-- Verifier chooses a disjunct. -/
  | orLeft {φ ψ : MuFormula Atom} {w : World} {st : List (MuFormula Atom)} :
      Move M ⟨.or φ ψ, w, st⟩ ⟨φ, w, st⟩
  /-- Verifier chooses a disjunct. -/
  ⋯

    def toMu : FixFreeFormula Atom → MuFormula Atom
  | .atom a => .atom a
  | .natom a => .natom a
  | .bot => .bot
  | .top => .top
  | .and φ ψ => .and (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .or φ ψ => .or (toMu φ) (toMu ψ)
  | .box φ => .box (toMu φ)
  | .dia φ => .dia (toMu φ)

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.losePlay

    For a fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi\) and a world \(w\), the lose play is the infinite sequence of positions of the fragment arena that is at the position \((\varphi, w)\) at time \(0\) and at the absorbing losing state at every later time. It is used for terminal losing positions \((\varphi, w)\).

    def losePlay (φ : FixFreeFormula Atom) (w : World) :
    ωSequence (FragPos Atom World) :=
  ⟨fun n => if n = 0 then FragPos.pos φ w else FragPos.lose⟩

  /-- Falsity. -/
  | bot : FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Truth. -/
  | top : FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Conjunction. -/
  | and : FixFreeFormula Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom → FixFreeFormula Atom
  /-- Disjunction. -/
  ⋯

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.losePlay_zero

    For a fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi\) and a world \(w\), the lose play from \((\varphi, w)\) is at the fragment-arena position \((\varphi, w)\) at time \(0\).

    theorem losePlay_zero (φ : FixFreeFormula Atom) (w : World) :
    losePlay φ w 0 = .pos φ w

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.losePlay_succ

    For every fixpoint-free formula \(\varphi\), world \(w\) and natural number \(n\), the lose play from \((\varphi, w)\) is at the losing state at time \(n + 1\).

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragPos_not_mem_winningRegion_of_terminal_lose

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\mathit{env}\) an environment assigning a set of worlds to each free-variable index, \(\varphi\) a fixpoint-free formula and \(w\) a world. If the model-checking game position \((\varphi, w, [])\), with \(\varphi\) embedded as a μ-calculus formula and the empty fixpoint stack, has no moves in \(M\) and is not a terminal win for Verifier in \(M\) under \(\mathit{env}\), then the embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) of the totalized fragment game of \(M\) and \(\mathit{env}\) is not in the winning region of the even player.

    The play that moves from \((\varphi, w)\) to the losing state and stays there conforms to every strategy of the even player and is won by the odd player.

    theorem fragPos_not_mem_winningRegion_of_terminal_lose {M : KripkeModel World Atom}
    {env : ℕ → World → Prop} {φ : FixFreeFormula Atom} {w : World}
    (hNo : NoMoves M ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩)
    (hLose : ¬ TerminalWin M env ⟨FixFreeFormula.toMu φ, w, []⟩) :
    (.pos φ w : FragPos Atom World) ∉
      (fragmentGame M env).WinningRegion Cslib.Games.Player.even
    structure KripkeModel (World : Type u) (Atom : Type v) where
  /-- There is at least one world. -/
  nonempty_world : Nonempty World
  /-- The accessibility relation. -/
  rel : World → World → Prop
  /-- The atoms true at each world. -/
  valuation : World → Atom → Prop

    CSLibExt/Logics/Modal/MuCalculusGame.lean:536

    def TerminalWin (M : KripkeModel World Atom) (env : ℕ → World → Prop) :
    GamePosition Atom World → Prop
  | ⟨.atom a, w, _⟩ => M.valuation w a
  | ⟨.natom a, w, _⟩ => ¬ M.valuation w a
  | ⟨.var n, w, st⟩ => if n < st.length then False else env (n - st.length) w
  | ⟨.top, _, _⟩ => True
  | ⟨.bot, _, _⟩ => False
  | ⟨.box _, _, _⟩ => True
  | ⟨.dia _, _, _⟩ => False
  | _ => False

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragment_canonical_to_wins

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\rho\) an environment, \(\varphi\) a fixpoint-free formula and \(w\) a world. If the embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) lies in the winning region of the even player in the fragment game of \(M\) and \(\rho\), then the backward-induction winning condition \(\mathrm{Wins}_M(\varphi, w)\) holds.

    The proof is by induction on \(\varphi\), using that the winning region is closed under the opponent's moves and under the moves of a winning strategy, so no strategy composition is needed.

    theorem fragment_canonical_to_wins (M : KripkeModel World Atom)
    (env : ℕ → World → Prop) (φ : FixFreeFormula Atom) (w : World)
    (h : (.pos φ w : FragPos Atom World) ∈
      (fragmentGame M env).WinningRegion Cslib.Games.Player.even) :
    Wins M φ w
    structure KripkeModel (World : Type u) (Atom : Type v) where
  /-- There is at least one world. -/
  nonempty_world : Nonempty World
  /-- The accessibility relation. -/
  rel : World → World → Prop
  /-- The atoms true at each world. -/
  valuation : World → Atom → Prop

    CSLibExt/Computability/Games/Parity.lean:56

    def Wins (M : KripkeModel World Atom) : FixFreeFormula Atom → World → Prop
  | .atom a, w => M.valuation w a
  | .natom a, w => ¬ M.valuation w a
  | .bot, _ => False
  | .top, _ => True
  | .and φ ψ, w => Wins M φ w ∧ Wins M ψ w
  | .or φ ψ, w => Wins M φ w ∨ Wins M ψ w
  | .box φ, w => ∀ v, M.rel w v → Wins M φ v
  | .dia φ, w => ∃ v, M.rel w v ∧ Wins M φ v

    Cslib.Modal.MuCalculus.Game.fragmentWins_iff_winningRegion

    Let \(M\) be a Kripke model, \(\rho\) an environment, \(\varphi\) a fixpoint-free formula and \(w\) a world. Then the backward-induction winning condition \(\mathrm{Wins}_M(\varphi, w)\) holds if and only if the embedded position \((\varphi, w)\) lies in the winning region of the even player in the fragment game of \(M\) and \(\rho\).

    theorem fragmentWins_iff_winningRegion (M : KripkeModel World Atom)
    (env : ℕ → World → Prop) (φ : FixFreeFormula Atom) (w : World) :
    Wins M φ w ↔ (.pos φ w : FragPos Atom World) ∈
      (fragmentGame M env).WinningRegion Cslib.Games.Player.even
